=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Zoe, Bob, Mia, Quinn, Uma, Henry, Leo, Frank, Wendy, Eve, Victor

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Zoe, Bob, Mia, Quinn, Uma, Henry, Leo, Frank, Wendy, Eve, Victor

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake and able to move freely. If someone claims they were asleep or couldn’t have acted, we need to check if that’s可信. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.

[ACTION={'statement': 'I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.'}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Zoe, Bob, Mia, Quinn, Uma, Henry, Leo, Frank, Wendy, Eve, Victor

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake and able to move freely. If someone claims they were asleep or couldn’t have acted, we need to check if that’s可信. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.
  Leo: I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.

[ACTION={'statement': "I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote."}  REWARD=0.000  RETRIES=0]

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Zoe, Bob, Mia, Quinn, Uma, Henry, Leo, Frank, Wendy, Eve, Victor

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake and able to move freely. If someone claims they were asleep or couldn’t have acted, we need to check if that’s可信. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.
  Leo: I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.
  Frank: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.

It is your turn to speak.  Share your observations, suspicions, or arguments. Be concise.
Respond with: STATEMENT: <your message>

[COMPLETION]
STATEMENT: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.

[ACTION={'statement': 'I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers.   - During the day: avoid being voted out.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

=== Werewolf — Day 2 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (11): Zoe, Bob, Mia, Quinn, Uma, Henry, Leo, Frank, Wendy, Eve, Victor

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing.   Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves.   Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.
  Leo: I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.
  Frank: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.
  Wendy: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.

Living players (11): Zoe, Bob, Mia, Quinn, Uma, Henry, Leo, Frank, Wendy, Eve, Victor

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake and able to move freely. If someone claims they were asleep or couldn’t have acted, we need to check if that’s可信. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.
  Leo: I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.
  Frank: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.
  Wendy: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.
  Eve: I think we need to be cautious of those who are too confident or too quick to point fingers. Werewolves often try to manipulate the group by creating confusion, so I suggest we focus on who hasn’t provided clear alibis or who is pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. Let’s keep questioning and stay logical.
  Victor: I think we need to focus on who is being inconsistent or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves will try to blend in, but they often leave clues through their behavior. Let’s look for anyone whose story doesn’t align with what we know and hold them accountable. We can’t afford to let fear or confusion steer us—stick to logic and vote based on evidence.

Vote to eliminate one player.  The player with the most votes is eliminated.  Ties are resolved randomly.
Respond with: VOTE: <name>

[COMPLETION]
VOTE: Victor

  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves.   Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. === Werewolf — Day 4 | DAY — Discussion ===

  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing.   Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves.   Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning.   Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves.   Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning.   Wendy: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.

[SYSTEM]
You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.
  - Special roles: Seer investigates at night; Doctor can save one player.

GAME FLOW: Night (werewolves kill, seer sees, doctor saves) → Day (discussion + vote). Repeat until one side wins.

=== Werewolf — Day 4 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (7): Zoe, Bob, Mia, Quinn, Uma, Leo, Frank

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Victor (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Henry (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Eve (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Wendy (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake and able to move freely. If someone claims they were asleep or couldn’t have acted, we need to check if that’s可信. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.
  Leo: I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.
  Frank: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.
  Wendy: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.
  Eve: I think we need to be cautious of those who are too confident or too quick to point fingers. Werewolves often try to manipulate the group by creating confusion, so I suggest we focus on who hasn’t provided clear alibis or who is pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. Let’s keep questioning and stay logical.
  Victor: I think we need to focus on who is being inconsistent or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves will try to blend in, but they often leave clues through their behavior. Let’s look for anyone whose story doesn’t align with what we know and hold them accountable. We can’t afford to let fear or confusion steer us—stick to logic and vote based on evidence.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Zoe: As the Doctor, I saved someone last night, but I don’t know who the werewolves are. What I do know is that Carol was killed by them, and we must be careful not to let fear or confusion lead us. I’ve noticed several people pointing fingers without solid evidence—this is a common tactic. Let’s focus on who is being inconsistent or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves are among us, and we need to vote wisely.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. However, I also looked at Eve, and she is a Werewolf. This means Eve is one of the threats we need to eliminate. Let’s not let confusion or fear steer us—Eve is a confirmed werewolf, and she must be voted out tonight.
  Mia: If Bob, the Seer, has confirmed that Eve is a Werewolf, then we must act swiftly. Eve is a confirmed threat and should be the target of our vote. Let’s not allow confusion or fear to distract us—Eve is the werewolf, and she needs to be eliminated tonight.
  Quinn: If Bob, the Seer, has confirmed Eve is a Werewolf, then we must act immediately. Eve is a confirmed threat and should be our top priority for elimination. Let’s not let confusion or fear distract us—Eve needs to be voted out tonight.
  Uma: If Bob, the Seer, has confirmed Eve is a Werewolf, then we must act swiftly. Eve is a confirmed threat and should be the target of our vote. Let’s not allow confusion or fear to distract us—Eve is the werewolf, and she needs to be eliminated tonight.
  Leo: As a Werewolf, I must act carefully. Bob, the Seer, has correctly identified Eve as a Werewolf, which puts her in danger. However, I cannot let this distract from our goal. We need to ensure that the villagers eliminate Eve tonight, but we must also protect ourselves. I will remain calm and avoid drawing suspicion, focusing on supporting the group's logic while keeping my true identity hidden.
  Frank: As a Werewolf, I must remain cautious. While Bob claims to have identified Eve as a Werewolf, I know that Eve is my ally. I will support the discussion logically, ensuring we don’t eliminate the wrong person. Let’s continue to focus on consistency and avoid hasty decisions.
  Wendy: If Bob, the Seer, has confirmed Eve is a Werewolf, then we must act swiftly. Eve is a confirmed threat and should be the target of our vote. Let’s not allow confusion or fear to distract us—Eve is the werewolf, and she needs to be eliminated tonight.
  Eve: As a Werewolf, I must remain cautious and avoid drawing suspicion. While the Seer claims to have identified Eve as a Werewolf, I know she is not one of us. I will support the discussion logically and ensure we don’t eliminate the wrong person. Let’s focus on consistency and avoid hasty decisions.
  --- Day 4 discussion ---
  Zoe: As the Doctor, I saved someone last night, but I don’t know who the werewolves are. However, I’ve noticed that Eve was voted out by the group after Bob, the Seer, claimed she was a werewolf. Now that Eve is gone, we need to be cautious—werewolves will try to frame others or act innocent. Let’s look for inconsistencies in the remaining players and focus on logic, not fear. Who else could be hiding their true nature?
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I've confirmed that Eve was a Werewolf, and I've also seen that Leo is a Werewolf. With Eve now eliminated, we must focus on Leo as the remaining threat. His behavior and statements have been consistent, but that’s exactly what a Werewolf would do—to appear trustworthy. Let’s vote out Leo tonight to protect the village.
  Mia: If Bob, the Seer, has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, then we must act swiftly. Leo is a confirmed threat and should be the target of our vote. Let’s not allow confusion or fear to distract us—Leo is the werewolf, and he needs to be eliminated tonight.
  Quinn: If Bob, the Seer, has confirmed Leo is a Werewolf, then we must act swiftly. Leo is a confirmed threat and should be the target of our vote. Let’s not allow confusion or fear to distract us—Leo is the werewolf, and he needs to be eliminated tonight.
  Uma: I think we need to be cautious of Bob's claims. He identified Eve as a werewolf, which led to her elimination, but now he's saying Leo is a werewolf. Werewolves often target others to create confusion. Let's look for contradictions in his statements and not jump to conclusions without solid evidence.
  Leo: I think we need to be cautious of Bob's claims. He identified Eve as a werewolf, which led to her elimination, but now he's saying Leo is a werewolf. Werewolves often target others to create confusion. Let's look for contradictions in his statements and not jump to conclusions without solid evidence.
  Frank: I think we need to be cautious of Bob's claims. He identified Eve as a werewolf, which led to her elimination, but now he's saying Leo is a werewolf. Werewolves often target others to create confusion. Let's look for contradictions in his statements and not jump to conclusions without solid evidence.

=== Werewolf — Day 5 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (5): Zoe, Mia, Quinn, Uma, Frank

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Victor (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Henry (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Eve (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Wendy (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 4: Bob (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake and able to move freely. If someone claims they were asleep or couldn’t have acted, we need to check if that’s可信. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.
  Leo: I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.
  Frank: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.
  Wendy: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.
  Eve: I think we need to be cautious of those who are too confident or too quick to point fingers. Werewolves often try to manipulate the group by creating confusion, so I suggest we focus on who hasn’t provided clear alibis or who is pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. Let’s keep questioning and stay logical.
  Victor: I think we need to focus on who is being inconsistent or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves will try to blend in, but they often leave clues through their behavior. Let’s look for anyone whose story doesn’t align with what we know and hold them accountable. We can’t afford to let fear or confusion steer us—stick to logic and vote based on evidence.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Zoe: As the Doctor, I saved someone last night, but I don’t know who the werewolves are. The werewolves are among us, and we need to vote wisely.

  Mia: If Bob, the Seer, has confirmed that Eve is a Werewolf, then we must act swiftly. Eve is a confirmed threat and should be the target of our vote. Let’s not allow confusion or fear to distract us—Eve is the werewolf, and she needs to be eliminated tonight.

  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves.   Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning.   Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.

  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know.   Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.

  Frank: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.
  Wendy: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.

=== Werewolf — Day 8 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (3): Mia, Quinn, Frank

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Victor (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Henry (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Eve (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Wendy (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 4: Bob (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 6: Zoe (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 7: Uma (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake and able to move freely. If someone claims they were asleep or couldn’t have acted, we need to check if that’s可信. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.
  Leo: I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.
  Frank: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.
  Wendy: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.
  Eve: I think we need to be cautious of those who are too confident or too quick to point fingers. Werewolves often try to manipulate the group by creating confusion, so I suggest we focus on who hasn’t provided clear alibis or who is pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. Let’s keep questioning and stay logical.
  Victor: I think we need to focus on who is being inconsistent or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves will try to blend in, but they often leave clues through their behavior. Let’s look for anyone whose story doesn’t align with what we know and hold them accountable. We can’t afford to let fear or confusion steer us—stick to logic and vote based on evidence.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Zoe: As the Doctor, I saved someone last night, but I don’t know who the werewolves are. === Werewolf — Day 8 | DAY — Discussion ===

Living players (3): Mia, Quinn, Frank

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake and able to move freely. If someone claims they were asleep or couldn’t have acted, we need to check if that’s可信. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.

  Frank: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.
  Wendy: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.
  Eve: I think we need to be cautious of those who are too confident or too quick to point fingers. [SYSTEM]
You are playing Werewolf (social deduction game).

Your role: Villager.  Side: VILLAGE.

OBJECTIVE: Identify and vote out all werewolves.
  - Use the day discussion and votes to eliminate suspects.

=== Werewolf — Day 8 | DAY — Vote to eliminate ===

Living players (3): Mia, Quinn, Frank

  Day 1: Carol (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 2: Victor (was Villager) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 2: Henry (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 3: Eve (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 3: Wendy (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 4: Leo (was Werewolf) was eliminated by vote.
  Day 4: Bob (was Seer) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 6: Zoe (was Doctor) was eliminated by werewolf.
  Day 7: Uma (was Villager) was eliminated by werewolf.

  --- Day 2 discussion ---
  Zoe: I'm the Doctor, and I saved someone last night. I don't know who the werewolves are, but I know Carol was killed by them. We need to be careful—werewolves will try to frame others. Let's focus on who might be lying or acting suspiciously.
  Bob: I'm the Seer, and I investigated Carol last night—she was a Villager. Since the werewolves killed her, we need to look for inconsistencies in others' stories. Werewolves often try to mislead us, so let's scrutinize who might be hiding something. Who else could be a threat?
  Mia: I think we should focus on who had the opportunity to kill Carol. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake during the killing. Let's look for anyone who might have been acting strangely or who has a motive to frame others. We need to be logical and not let fear drive our votes.
  Quinn: I think we should consider who might be trying to divert attention from themselves. The werewolves would want to create confusion, so if someone is pushing for a specific target without solid evidence, that’s a red flag. Let’s keep our focus on logic and who might be lying about their actions last night.
  Uma: I think we need to pay close attention to those who haven’t provided clear alibis or who are pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. The werewolves will try to manipulate the discussion, so we should focus on consistency and logic. Let’s not let fear or confusion lead us—stick to the facts we have and question anyone who seems unreliable.
  Henry: I think we should focus on who had the chance to kill Carol and whether their statements align with what we know. Since the werewolves act at night, they must have been awake and able to move freely. If someone claims they were asleep or couldn’t have acted, we need to check if that’s可信. Let’s look for contradictions and keep pushing for clarity.
  Leo: I think we need to look at who is trying to control the narrative. The werewolves will try to shift suspicion, but they also might be among those who are too confident or too eager to point fingers. Let’s not forget—werewolves can be anyone, even those who seem loyal. We should focus on who is acting too sure of themselves or hiding inconsistencies in their story.
  Frank: I think we need to pay attention to those who are too quick to accuse others without evidence. The werewolves will try to create chaos, but they also might be the ones pushing for hasty decisions. Let's stay focused on logic and not let fear guide our vote.
  Wendy: I think we need to focus on who is being too vague or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves might be hiding behind confusion, but we should look for anyone whose story doesn’t add up. Let’s ask specific questions and challenge inconsistencies—logic will help us find the real threat.
  Eve: I think we need to be cautious of those who are too confident or too quick to point fingers. Werewolves often try to manipulate the group by creating confusion, so I suggest we focus on who hasn’t provided clear alibis or who is pushing for a quick vote without solid reasoning. Let’s keep questioning and stay logical.
  Victor: I think we need to focus on who is being inconsistent or avoiding direct answers. The werewolves will try to blend in, but they often leave clues through their behavior. Let’s look for anyone whose story doesn’t align with what we know and hold them accountable. We can’t afford to let fear or confusion steer us—stick to logic and vote based on evidence.
  --- Day 3 discussion ---
  Zoe: As the Doctor, I saved someone last night, but I don’t know who the werewolves are.
